## 應用模板布局 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 布局工具集

**english**　Apply Template Layouts

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/apply-template-layouts.html

**id**　4cc378ed46f42c706f-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/apply-template-layouts.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Apply Template Layouts，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網絡邏輯示意圖圖層 [in_network_diagram_layer]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/apply-template-layouts.html#parameters

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。

消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 轉為浮點型 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4cd8eac7ccc47865bc-2

**english**　Float

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 數學分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html

**text**　輸入值可為正數，也可為負數。
如果對已經是浮點型的輸入執行轉為浮點型操作，則輸出值將與輸入值相同。
如果輸入是多波段網格，則輸出也將為多波段網格。 該工具將在輸入中的每個波段上執行操作。
如果輸入是 多維網格資料，則將處理所有變量的所有切片。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html#usage

**text**　# Name: Float_3d_Ex_02.py
# Description: Converts each cell value of a raster into a floating-point
#    representation
# Requirements: 3D Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "landuse"

# Run Float
arcpy.ddd.Float(inRaster, "C:/output/outfloat")

## 轉為浮點型 (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 數學分析工具集

**english**　Float

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html

**id**　4cd8eac7ccc47865bc-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Float，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格資料或常量值 [in_raster_or_constant]、輸出網格 [out_raster]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/float.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**text**　Extracts features from multiple input feature classes into a target database.
This tool can extract all features, only the schema, or features that match filter criteria. Features can be filtered based on a polygon feature. The Target Geodatabase parameter can be a database containing input schema, or it can be an empty database in which the tool will create a schema that matches the input dataset.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#summary

## Extract Data By Feature (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　4ce1df20916a25b0f8-2

**english**　Extract Data By Feature

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**text**　The Input Datasets parameter supports points, polylines, polygons, and annotation features.
If the input features include a topology, features are extracted from all the layers that participate in that topology. This may be in addition to the layers specified in the Input Datasets parameter.
The extracted data will use the same names as the data provided for the Input Datasets parameter.
All Input Datasets parameter values must originate from the same workspace. For example, if the extracted data originates from a feature dataset, the tool will create a feature dataset of the same name in the target geodatabase.
The target geodatabase must exist before you run this tool.
The Re-use Schema parameter is enabled for file geodatabases only.
The Filter Feature Layer parameter requires that one feature is selected.
The selected feature in the Filter Feature Layer parameter must be a polygon. If it is not, the tool will return an error.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#usage

## Extract Data By Feature (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　4ce1df20916a25b0f8-3

**english**　Extract Data By Feature

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**text**　If the target geodatabase is a Microsoft SQL Server Express database and some of the input datasets are open in ArcGIS Pro, the extraction may fail due to schema locks.
If a replica with the same name exists in either the source or target geodatabase, the operation will fail and the tool will return an error.
If you are creating a checkout replica, the input datasets must be registered as versioned.
Web feature services are not supported for extraction at this time.
Enterprise databases are not supported as a Target Geodatabase parameter value at this time.
Related data is extracted by default. To avoid extracting related data, you must specify each relationship class that defines the related objects you want to exclude using the Excluded Relationship Classes parameter.
Related data is extracted in a forward direction, from origin to destination.
The Expression parameter value will be applied to all Input Datasets parameter values and the fields used in the SQL expression must exist in all tables. If they don't, the tool will fail and report the name of the tables that don't satisfy the expression. This also applies to feature layers with invalid definition queries.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#usage

**text**　The SQL query build drop-down options in the Expression parameter will contain a list of the fields that are shared by all of the Input Datasets parameter values.
When the Checkout replica parameter is checked, the Filter Spatial Relation parameter only supports the Intersects and Contains options. The Filter Spatial Relation parameter's Clip option is only supported when the Checkout replica parameter is unchecked.

**parameter**　in_datasets

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Input Datasets
- A value table of rows that contain a dataset to extract and a filter option for that dataset. Specifying a filter option allows you to control how rows are replicated in each dataset. 值表列： Dataset—The schema of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Rows Option—Specifies whether all rows, only the schema, or features that match filter criteria will be extracted. All Rows—All rows of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Schema Only—Only the schema of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Use Filters—If a feature layer is specified for the Filter Feature Layer parameter, features that either intersect or are contained by features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted.
- Value Table

**id**　4ce1df20916a25b0f8-10

**english**　Extract Data By Feature

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**parameter**　filter_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Filter Spatial Relation （可選）
- Specifies the spatial relationship between the Filter Feature Layer and Input Datasets parameter values and how that relationship will be filtered. The spatial relationship is applied to data in an extent defined by the area of interest (AOI) specified in the Filter Feature Layer parameter. Intersects—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that intersect features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted. Contains—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that are contained by the selected feature in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted. Clip—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that intersect features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted, the features will be split at the AOI boundary, and only the features within the AOI will be kept.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**parameter**　expand_feature_classes_and_tables

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Expand Feature Classes and Tables （可選）
- Specifies whether expanded feature classes and tables—such as those in networks, topologies, or relationship classes—will be added. Use defaults—The expanded feature classes and tables related to the feature classes and tables in the replica will be added. The default for feature classes is to replicate all features intersecting the spatial filter. If no spatial filter has been provided, all features are included. The default for tables is to replicate only the schema. Add with schema only—Only the schema for the expanded feature classes and tables will be added. All rows—All rows for expanded feature classes and tables will be added. Do not add—No expanded feature classes and tables will be added. This is the default.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**parameter**　filter_type

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- filter_type （可選）
- Specifies the spatial relationship between the filter_feature and in_datasets parameter values and how that relationship will be filtered. The spatial relationship is applied to data in an extent defined by the area of interest (AOI) specified in the filter_feature parameter. INTERSECTS—Features in the in_datasets parameter value that intersect features in the filter_feature parameter value will be extracted. CONTAINS—Features in the in_datasets parameter value that are contained by the selected feature in the filter_feature parameter value will be extracted. CLIP—Features in the in_datasets parameter value that intersect features in the filter_feature parameter value will be extracted, the features will be split at the AOI boundary, and only the features within the AOI will be kept.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#derived-output

**parameter**　expand_feature_classes_and_tables

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- expand_feature_classes_and_tables （可選）
- Specifies whether expanded feature classes and tables—such as those in networks, topologies, or relationship classes—will be added. USE_DEFAULTS—The expanded feature classes and tables related to the feature classes and tables in the replica will be added. The default for feature classes is to replicate all features intersecting the spatial filter. If no spatial filter has been provided, all features are included. The default for tables is to replicate only the schema. ADD_WITH_SCHEMA_ONLY—Only the schema for the expanded feature classes and tables will be added. ALL_ROWS—All rows for expanded feature classes and tables will be added. DO_NOT_ADD—No expanded feature classes and tables will be added. This is the default.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**text**　ExtractDataByFeature example (stand-alone script)
The following stand-alone script demonstrates how to use the ExtractDataByFeature function to extract features from an input feature class into a target file geodatabase.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#derived-output

## Extract Data By Feature (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　4ce1df20916a25b0f8-32

**english**　Extract Data By Feature

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**text**　# Name: ExtractDataByFeature_sample.py
# Description: Extracts features from an input feature class into a target file geodatabase.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Making AOI Feature Layer
Index = r'C:\Data\ReferenceData\MapIndex.gdb\MapIndex\TM50_Index'
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(Index, "TM50_Index")

# Selecting a single AOI from the AOI feature layer
selection_string = "NRN = 'V795X16573'"
arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute("TM50_Index", "NEW_SELECTION", selection_string)

# Setting Local Variables
in_datasets = r'C:\Data\TDS_7.gdb\TDS\StructurePnt'
target_gdb = r'C:\Data\ExtractTest.gdb'
reuse_schema = "DO_NOT_REUSE"
filter_feature = "TM50_Index"
filter_type = "CONTAINS"
checkout_replica = "DO_NOT_CHECKOUT_REPLICA"
replica_name = ''
expand_feature_classes_and_tables = 'DO_NOT_ADD'
get_related_data = 'DO_NOT_GET_RELATED'
excluded_rel_classes = None
where_clause = ''

# Calling the Extract Data By Feature tool to extract Structure Point features to a target file geodatabase
arcpy.topographic.ExtractDataByFeature(in_datasets, target_gdb, reuse_schema, filter_feature, filter_type, checkout_replica, replica_name, expand_feature_classes_and_tables, get_related_data, excluded_rel_classes, where_clause)

# Getting all messages, warnings, and errors from the tool run and printing the results back to the user
messages = arcpy.GetMessages(0)
warnings = arcpy.GetMessages(1)
errors = arcpy.GetMessages(2)
arcpy.AddMessage('Tool Messages: {}\nTool Warnings: {}\nTool Errors{}\n'.format(messages, warnings, errors))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#derived-output

## Extract Data By Feature (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**english**　Extract Data By Feature

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html

**id**　4ce1df20916a25b0f8-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Extract Data By Feature，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Datasets [in_datasets]、Target Geodatabase [target_gdb]、Re-use Schema （可選） [reuse_schema]、Filter Feature Layer （可選） [filter_feature]、Filter Spatial Relation （可選） [filter_type]、Checkout replica （可選） [checkout_replica]、Replica Name （可選） [replica_name]、Expand Feature Classes and Tables （可選） [expand_feature_classes_and_tables]、Replicate Related Data （可選） [get_related_data]、Excluded Relationship Classes （可選） [excluded_rel_classes]、Expression （可選） [where_clause]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/topographic-production/extract-data-by-feature.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_datasets", "label_zh": "Input Datasets", "label_en": "Input Datasets", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "A value table of rows that contain a dataset to extract and a filter option for that dataset. Specifying a filter option allows you to control how rows are replicated in each dataset. Value table columns: Dataset—The schema of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Rows Option—Specifies whether all rows, only the schema, or features that match filter criteria will be extracted. All Rows—All rows of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Schema Only—Only the schema of the dataset will be extracted to the child workspace. Use Filters—If a feature layer is specified for the Filter Feature Layer parameter, features that either intersect or are contained by features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted."}, {"parameter": "target_gdb", "label_zh": "Target Geodatabase", "label_en": "Target Geodatabase", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Workspace; GeoDataServer", "description_en": "The workspace into which data will be extracted."}, {"parameter": "reuse_schema", "label_zh": "Re-use Schema （可選）", "label_en": "Re-use Schema (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the schema of the data that will be extracted will be reused. This reduces the amount of time required to extract the data. This parameter is only supported for file geodatabases. Checked—The schema will be reused. Unchecked—The schema will not be reused. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "filter_feature", "label_zh": "Filter Feature Layer （可選）", "label_en": "Filter Feature Layer (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Layer", "description_en": "A feature layer with one selected feature that will be used to limit the extent of the extracted data."}, {"parameter": "filter_type", "label_zh": "Filter Spatial Relation （可選）", "label_en": "Filter Spatial Relation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the spatial relationship between the Filter Feature Layer and Input Datasets parameter values and how that relationship will be filtered. The spatial relationship is applied to data in an extent defined by the area of interest (AOI) specified in the Filter Feature Layer parameter. Intersects—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that intersect features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted. Contains—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that are contained by the selected feature in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted. Clip—Features in the Input Datasets parameter value that intersect features in the Filter Feature Layer parameter value will be extracted, the features will be split at the AOI boundary, and only the features within the AOI will be kept."}, {"parameter": "checkout_replica", "label_zh": "Checkout replica （可選）", "label_en": "Checkout replica (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the replica will be checked out, replicated, edited, and checked back in one time. Checked—The replica will be checked out. Unchecked—The replica will not be checked out. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "replica_name", "label_zh": "Replica Name （可選）", "label_en": "Replica Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the replica that will be checked out."}, {"parameter": "expand_feature_classes_and_tables", "label_zh": "Expand Feature Classes and Tables （可選）", "label_en": "Expand Feature Classes and Tables (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether expanded feature classes and tables—such as those in networks, topologies, or relationship classes—will be added. Use defaults—The expanded feature classes and tables related to the feature classes and tables in the replica will be added. The default for feature classes is to replicate all features intersecting the spatial filter. If no spatial filter has been provided, all features are included. The default for tables is to replicate only the schema. Add with schema only—Only the schema for the expanded feature classes and tables will be added. All rows—All rows for expanded feature classes and tables will be added. Do not add—No expanded feature classes and tables will be added. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "get_related_data", "label_zh": "Replicate Related Data （可選）", "label_en": "Replicate Related Data (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether rows related to existing rows in the replica will be replicated. For example, consider a feature (f1) inside the replication filter and a related feature (f2) from another class outside the filter. Feature f2 will be included in the replica if you choose to get related data. Checked—Related data will be replicated. Unchecked—Related data will not be replicated. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "excluded_rel_classes", "label_zh": "Excluded Relationship Classes （可選）", "label_en": "Excluded Relationship Classes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Relationship Class", "description_en": "The relationship classes with the relationships to exclude from extraction. The relationship classes will still be included if both datasets that participate are present, but the related objects are not extracted."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "Expression （可選）", "label_en": "Expression (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that is used to further refine results from the AOI extraction."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.topographic.ExtractDataByFeature(in_datasets, target_gdb, {reuse_schema}, {filter_feature}, {filter_type}, {checkout_replica}, {replica_name}, {expand_feature_classes_and_tables}, {get_related_data}, {excluded_rel_classes}, {where_clause})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_datasets、target_gdb、reuse_schema、filter_feature、filter_type、checkout_replica、replica_name、expand_feature_classes_and_tables、get_related_data、excluded_rel_classes、where_clause。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 120135：計算新欄位值時，出現一個或多個錯誤。 · 解決方案

**id**　4ceaaa78417d32a6b1-2

**english**　120135: One or more errors occurred while calculating the new field values.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 120101-120200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/120001-130000/tool-errors-and-warnings-120126-120150-120135.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/120001-130000/tool-errors-and-warnings-120126-120150-120135.html

**text**　請確保表達式中使用的欄位或值是傳遞到函數中的正確資料類型。 請驗證這些欄位或值不包含可能導致計算無效的零值或 null 值（即，除以 0）。 請驗證函數是否符合正確的 Arcade 表達式語法。

## 拓撲驗證 (資料管理工具) · 匯總

**id**　4cef858743e71711cb-1

**english**　Validate Topology

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 拓撲工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html

**text**　驗證地理資料庫拓撲。
該工具用于執行下列操作：
對圖徵折點進行裂化和聚類以查找共享幾何（具有通用座標）的圖徵
將通用座標折點插入到共享幾何的圖徵中
運行一系列完整性檢查以確定是否違反了為拓撲定義的規則

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#summary

## 拓撲驗證 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　4cef858743e71711cb-2

**english**　Validate Topology

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 拓撲工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html

**text**　可以使用范圍環境并選中使用范圍環境參數來驗證拓撲的特定部分。
此工具將只處理臟區。 要了解有關臟區的詳細信息，請參閱 ArcGIS 中的拓撲。
從 ArcGIS Pro 2.6 開始，如果拓撲服務是與 ArcGIS Enterprise 10.8.1 或更高版本一起發布的，則輸入拓撲圖層可以來自該服務。 如果該圖層已添加至內容窗格，您可以將圖層拖動到輸入拓撲參數，也可以提供拓撲圖層圖徵服務的 URL，例如 https://myserver.mydomain.com/server/rest/services/myTopoService/FeatureServer/0。
當輸入拓撲來自圖徵服務時，此工具將異步執行驗證過程。 許可： 在 ArcGIS Enterprise 11.2 或更高版本的部署中使用分支版本化時，必須為組織成員分配 ArcGIS Advanced Editing 用戶類型擴展模塊的許可，才能使用此工具。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#usage

## 拓撲驗證 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4cef858743e71711cb-14

**english**　Validate Topology

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 拓撲工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html

**text**　# Name: ValidateTopology_Example.py
# Description: Validates a topology using the environment extent (visible map display)

import arcpy
processing_extent = arcpy.Extent(
    2357129.94288309, 1424105.53783632, 2573455.6694351, 1663482.36639798,
    spatial_reference=arcpy.SpatialReference(102758, vcs=5703)
)

# Use EnvManager to temporarily set the Extent environment for the duration of
# the with block.
with arcpy.EnvManager(extent=processing_extent):
    arcpy.management.ValidateTopology("D:/Calgary/Transport.gdb/Streets/Street_Topo")

## 拓撲驗證 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱

**english**　Validate Topology

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html

**id**　4cef858743e71711cb-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Validate Topology，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入拓撲 [in_topology]、使用范圍環境 （可選） [visible_extent]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/validate-topology.html#parameters

## 010200：PATHDISTANCE：垂直系數參數文件不含任何資料。 · 描述

**id**　4cf3cff6ee2c56c0d3-1

**english**　010200: PATHDISTANCE: No data in the vertical factor parameter file.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 10101-10200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10176-10200-010200.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10176-10200-010200.html

**text**　所輸入的垂直系數參數文件不含資料或采用了 PathDistance 無法接受的格式。 垂直系數圖用于定義垂直成本系數 (VF) 和垂直相對移動角度 (VRMA) 之間的關系。 垂直參數文件是一個 ASCII 文件，用于生成圖表。 該文件應包含兩列。 第一列以度為單位標識 VRMA，第二列為 VF。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10176-10200-010200.html#description

- 數學分析工具集

**text**　返回網格中每個像元的最近的較小整數值（以浮點表示）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#summary

## 下舍入 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4cf50375d7f001990e-2

**english**　Round Down

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html

**text**　輸入值可為正數，也可為負數。
無論輸入值是何種類型，此工具的輸出網格資料始終為浮點型。
如果輸入數字在小數點右側包含任何數值，則會為輸出分配最近的較小整數值，并以不含任何小數部分的浮點數表示。 例如： Input Output 5.3 5.0 4.9 4.0 3.0 3.0 6.5 6.0 -0.2 -1.0 -2.8 -3.0
轉為整型工具與下舍入工具之間存在差別。 例如，對于以下兩個值，轉為整型工具總是截斷數字： 1.5 將變為 1 -1.5 將變為 -1 而對于相同的兩個值，下舍入工具將返回如下： 1.5 將變為 1.0 -1.5 將變為 -2.0 另一個差別在于下舍入工具輸出浮點值，而轉為整型工具僅輸出整數值。
如果輸入是多波段網格，則輸出也將為多波段網格。 該工具將在輸入中的每個波段上執行操作。
如果輸入是多維網格資料，則將處理所有變量的所有切片。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。
有關適用于此工具的地理處理環境的詳細信息，請參閱分析環境和 Spatial Analyst。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#usage

## 下舍入 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4cf50375d7f001990e-12

**english**　Round Down

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html

**text**　# Name: RoundDown_Ex_02.py
# Description: Returns the next lower whole number for each cell in a raster
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "gwhead"

# Run RoundDown
outRoundDRaster = RoundDown(inRaster)

# Save the output
outRoundDRaster.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/outrounddown")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#return-value

## 下舍入 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**english**　Round Down

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html

**id**　4cf50375d7f001990e-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Round Down，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格資料或常量值 [in_raster_or_constant]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/round-down.html#parameters

## 移除連接 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　4d2c3b6e59d9e035f2-2

**english**　Remove Join

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 連接和關聯工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html

**text**　連接參數值是連接到輸入圖層或表視圖的表的名稱。 如果連接表是名為 MyTable.dbf 的 dBASE 文件，則連接名稱將是 MyTable；因此，要移除該連接，請指定 MyTable。 如果連接表是地理資料庫或名為 MyTable2 的 INFO 表，則連接名稱將是 MyTable2；因此，要移除該連接，請指定 MyTable2。 連接名稱不會反映表視圖本身的名稱，而是反映表視圖的源。 例如，如果表視圖名為 TableView1 并指向 mytable.dbf，則連接的名稱將是 mytable。
如果圖層連接到兩個表并且移除了第一個連接，則兩個連接都將被移除。 例如，將 Layer1 連接到 TableA。 然后將 Layer1 連接到 TableB。 如果移除了 TableA 的連接，則將一并移除 TableB 的連接。
在 ModelBuilder 中，可使用創建圖徵圖層工具通過圖徵類創建圖層，創建表視圖工具可通過輸入表或圖徵類創建表視圖。 這些圖層或表視圖可隨后用作添加連接和移除連接工具的輸入。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html#usage

## 移除連接 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4d2c3b6e59d9e035f2-14

**english**　Remove Join

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 連接和關聯工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html

**text**　# AddFieldFromJoin.py
# Description: Add a field to a table, and calculate its values based
#              on the values in a field from a joined table

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# set the environments
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"
arcpy.env.qualifiedFieldNames = "UNQUALIFIED"

# Define script parameters
inFeatures = "Habitat_Analysis.gdb/vegtype"
layerName = "veg_layer"
newField = "description"
joinTable = "vegtable.dbf"
joinField = "HOLLAND95"
calcExpression = "!vegtable.VEG_TYPE!"
outFeature = "Habitat_Analysis.gdb/vegjoin335"

# Add the new field
arcpy.management.AddField(inFeatures, newField, "TEXT")

# Create a feature layer from the vegtype feature class
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(inFeatures,  layerName)

# Join the feature layer to a table
arcpy.management.AddJoin(layerName, joinField, joinTable, joinField)

# Populate the newly created field with values from the joined table
arcpy.management.CalculateField(layerName, newField, calcExpression, "PYTHON")

# Remove the join
arcpy.management.RemoveJoin(layerName, "vegtable")

# Copy the layer to a new permanent feature class
arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(layerName, outFeature)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html#derived-output

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-join.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Remove Join，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。圖層名稱或表視圖 [in_layer_or_view]、連接 （可選） [join_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

## Load Data (Topographic Production Tools) · 匯總

**id**　4d3a51ddb567d31746-1

**english**　Load Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html

**text**　Moves features from one schema to another by loading data from a source to a target workspace. Data mapping rules described in a cross-reference database are applied during loading.
Data that matches the schema defined in the cross-reference database for the source is appended to the target workspace. The cross-reference database contains the DatasetMapping table that lists pairs of source and target dataset names. Each source and target name pair can have a WHERE clause and a subtype. The WHERE clause defines a subset of features in the source to append to the target. Subtype identifies a subtype in the target feature class into which features are loaded.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#summary

## Load Data (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　4d3a51ddb567d31746-3

**english**　Load Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html

**text**　The cross-reference database includes other tables that affect load data functionality. These tables enable the following operations: Map fields between data sources. Override values from source to target. Specify different source and target schema owners for enterprise geodatabases.
Use the Dataset Mapping Definitions parameter to specify the feature classes or shapefiles to load from the input source to the target workspace. The list of sources and targets comes from the DatasetMapping table in the cross-reference database.
You can only set WHERE clauses and subtypes in the DatasetMapping table in the cross-reference database. You cannot set them with the Dataset Mapping Definitions parameter.
The ID used in the Dataset Mapping Definitions parameter must refer to an existing row ID in the DatasetMapping table in the cross-reference database. The row in the DatasetMapping table contains the name of the source and target datasets.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#usage

## Load Data (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　4d3a51ddb567d31746-20

**english**　Load Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html

**text**　# Name: LoadData_sample.py
# Description: Loading data from one geodatabase to another using a cross-reference geodatabase

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting Local Variables
cross_reference = r'C:\Temp\Cross_Reference.mdb'
source_database = r'C:\Temp\Source.gdb'
target_database = r'C:\Temp\Target.gdb'

# Loading data from the source to the target geodatabase
arcpy.topographic.LoadData(cross_reference, source_database, target_database)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#derived-output

## Load Data (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 地理資料庫工具集

**english**　Load Data

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html

**id**　4d3a51ddb567d31746-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Load Data，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Cross-reference Database [in_cross_reference]、Input Sources [in_sources]、Input Target Workspace [in_target]、Dataset Mapping Definitions （可選） [in_dataset_map_defs]、Log row level errors （可選） [row_level_errors]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/load-data.html#parameters

## 003531：參數 <value>（<value> 工具中）的值對于其新的 dataType 不再有效，工具可能無法正確運行 · 描述

**id**　4d488905a1a54f0ddb-1

**english**　003531: Parameter <value> on the <value> tool has a value that is no longer is valid against its new dataType, the tool may not run correctly

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 3501-3600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03526-03550-003531.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03526-03550-003531.html

**text**　分析器使用工具參數標識了一個兼容性問題。
工具參數具有用于定義有效值以及這些值的驗證的 dataType 屬性。 這在使用模型時特別重要。
參數已更改，因此模型中的值與目標版本中參數的 dataType 屬性不兼容。
例如，在 ArcGIS Pro 3.6 中，工具的參數采用圖徵類或網格的 dataType 屬性，且在將此參數值設為網格的情況下創建模型。 然后，模型將保存到參數的 dataType 屬性為圖徵類的較早版本。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03526-03550-003531.html#description
